1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any hydrocarbons module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 98. The projective resolution has length at most 861 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 98 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the hydrocarbons structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 861. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
